Objective for c:
```
def lbf_b_obj(c, n, beta, scale, state):
    z = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    z[:-1]=c
    z[-1]=0.0
    m = np.max(z)
    ez = np.exp(z-m)
    s = ez.sum()
    p = ez/s
    b = fft_convolution(p)
    G, w = smooth_max_and_weights(b,beta)
    g = 2.0 * corr_pw(p,w)
    grad_z = p * (g - np.dot(p,g))
    grad_c = grad_z[:-1].copy()
    F = scale * float(n * G)
    if not finite: return large, zeros, update? 
    # track best using true score? use n*G? For official score factor 2. track 2n*max(b) maybe.
    if score < state['best']: state['best]=score; state['best_p']=p.copy(); state['best_c']=c.copy()
    return F, grad_c
```

Let's implement exact gradient functions:

```
def fft_conv(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0)
    need=2*n-1
    size=1
    while size<need: size <<=1
    F=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    b=np.fft.irfft(F*F,size)[:need]
    # FFT may produce tiny negative; for max, negative fine? For nonnegative exact, max unaffected if tiny positive? But for convex? Use max(b,0) to avoid negative? If tiny negative at endpoints, max not; okay. But gradient maybe weights negative? We use b only for max, negative ignored.
    return np.maximum(b,0)
```
Because b exact nonnegative; clipping negative roundoff okay. If true values near zero, clipping increases? negligible.

`smooth_max_and_weights(b,beta)`:
- m=max(b).
- if m<=0 or beta<=0: w=ones/len.
- x=beta*(b-m); clip [-745,0]; e=exp; Z=sum; if Z<=0 fallback to argmax.
- w=e/Z; G=m+log(Z)/beta.

```
def grad_block_or_full(p,w,n):

    g=2*s
    gp=np.dot(p,g)
    return g, gp
```

`corr_pw(p,w)` as previous:
```
def corr_pw(p,w):
    n=p.size; m=w.size
    if n==0 or m==0: return zeros
    need=n+m-1; size next pow2
    P=fftp.rfft(p,size); WR=fftp.rfft(w[::-1],size); C=irfft(P*WR,size)[:need]
    s = C[m-n:m][::-1]
    return s
```
Check for n=1: need=1+m-1=m; C? m-n=0 to m -> C[0:m][::-1]; s0 should p0 sum w? C? p0*w[::-1] convolution? Let's test p=[p0], w length m. C[k]=p0*w[k]. C[0:m][::-1]=[w[m-1]p0,...,w0p0]; s0=w[m-1]p0 but desired sum_j p_j w_{0+j}=p0*(sum w?) Wait s_i = sum_j p_j w_{i+j}. For n=1,i=0: want p0 * Σ_{j=0}^0? w_j? w_j only j? w length 2n-1? For full, w length need=2n-1=1? m=1. In general n=1,m=1. Good. n=2,m=3. We previously formula works. For block p construction:
```
def block_to_p(n,B,c):
    L=n//B
    z=np.zeros(B); z[:-1]=c; # last zero for softmax? q=softmax(z)
    q = softmax(z)
    p=np.repeat(q/L, L)
    return p
```
If q sum1. This parameterization B variables B-1. Gradient from p is grad_p length n; aggregate to block: gblocks = np.sum(reshape(g,B,L),axis=1). grad_z = q*(gblocks - dot(q,gblocks)). Objective if using c with last fixed zero: grad_c=grad_z[:-1]. Good.

Block objective function for L-BFGS:
```
def block_objective(c,n,B,beta,scale,state):
   p=block_to_p(...)
   b=fft_conv(p)
   G,w=smooth...
   g=2*corr_pw(p,w)
   gb=g.reshape(B,L).sum(axis=1)
   gp=np.dot(p,g) # also =np.dot(q,gb)
   grad_z=q*(gb-np.dot(q,gb))
   grad_c=grad_z[:-1]
   F=scale*n*G + reg*dot(c,c)
   track score=2*n*max(b)
   return F, grad_c
```

Projected gradient on p simplex:
```
def projected_momentum_refine(p0, beta, deadline, start_step=1.0):
    p=normalize_simplex(p0)
    y=p
    mu=0.9 maybe adapt
    best=score
    step=1.0
    for it until deadline:
        b=fft_conv(p); G,w; cur
        if improve best
        g=2*corr(p,w); grad=g - mean(p? projection uses gradient in Euclidean with constraint sum=1, subtract component along 1. Since feasible affine sum1, gradient projected = g - np.dot(p,g) (because p normal? Actually for simplex, gradient of L wrt p, projected step p - step*(g - dot(p,g))).)
        line search with step multipliers; maybe use backtracking to decrease smooth objective.
        q = project_simplex(p - step*grad)
        if accepted: y=(q-mu*(q-p_prev))? FISTA? For nonconvex, use heavy-ball: new = q + mu*(q-p_prev). project.
```
This is time-safe. But L-BFGS likely better. We can use projected gradient if no scipy? Could. Implement to utilize residual time.

But momentum with smooth max beta maybe. Use heavy-ball μ=0.8, step adapt. Could improve. Need avoid oscillation. Use simple gradient with backtracking (as previous full_refine) maybe good enough. We can implement FISTA-style:
```
y = p
t=1
for:
  grad_y = grad(y)
  q=project(y - step*grad_y)
  t_new=(1+sqrt(1+4*t^2))/2
```

```
def get_height_sequence_1():
    h = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
    if h is None: return None
    try: arr=np.asarray(h,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except: return None
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), np.maximum(arr,0.0), 0.0)
    s=arr.sum()
    if s<=0 or arr.size<2: return None
    return arr/s
```

```
def generate_basic_starts(n, rng):
   starts=[]

   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   exp ramps: for a in [-4,-2,-1,1,2,4]: p=np.exp(a*x); p/sum
   beta: for a,b in combos: p=x**a*(1-x)**b; if >min
   half: for split fraction in [0.25,0.5,0.75], ratio [0.2,0.5,0.8]
   sine: p=1+0.6*sin(2π k x+φ), φ=...; clip
   random smooth: for freqs in [4,8,16,32]: z=fftlow; p=exp(1.5*tanh(z))
   return starts
```

```
       for i in range(1,len(cands)):
```

L-BFGS objective with state and globals:
```
def full_objective(c, n, beta, scale, state):
    global GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE, GLOBAL_BEST_P
    if c is None: return 1e30, np.zeros(max(n-1,0))
    c = np.asarray(c,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if c.size != n-1:
        tmp = np.zeros(n-1); m=min(tmp.size,c.size); tmp[:m]=c[:m]; c=tmp
    z = np.empty(n); z[:-1]=c; z[-1]=0.0
    m_z = np.max(z)
    ez = np.exp(z-m_z)
    s = ez.sum()
    if not finite or s<=0: return 1e30, zeros
    p = ez/s
    b = fft_convolution(p)
    G,w = smooth_max_and_weights(b,beta)
    if not finite G: return 1e30, zeros
    g = 2.0*corr_pw(p,w)
    gp = float(np.dot(p,g))
    grad_z = p * (g - gp)
    grad = grad_z[:-1]
    # scale
    F = scale * float(n*G)
    grad = scale * grad
    # optional ridge
    # reg = 1e-12? skip
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)): return 1e30, zeros
    score = 2.0 * n * float(np.max(b))
    if np.isfinite(score):
       state['traj_score']=score; state['traj_p']=p.copy(); state['traj_c']=c.copy()
       if score < GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE:
           GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE=score; GLOBAL_BEST_P=normalize_simplex(p)
    return F, grad
```
Need `np.max(b)` if b empty? n>0. Good.

`block_objective` similar. It constructs p from c:

```
...
g=2*corr_pw(p,w)
gb=g.reshape(B,L).sum(axis=1)
gq=float(np.dot(q,gb))
grad_z=q*(gb-gq)
grad_c=grad_z[:-1]
F=scale*float(n*G) # scale=B
grad=scale*grad_c
score=2*n*max(b)
update state/global
```

```
def gradient_refine_full(p0, deadline, beta=None, beta_max? start_beta=10*n):
    p=normalize_simplex(p0); n=p.size
    if beta is None: beta=max(10000,10*n)
    best=p, best_score=fast_score
    step=1.0
    prev=p
    mu=0.0 (maybe no momentum to avoid divergence)
    no_improve=0
    for it while time<deadline:
       b=fft_conv(p); G,w=smooth...
       cur_score=n*G exact? Use smooth for line search.
       g=2*corr_pw(p,w); grad=g - dot(p,g)
       # backtracking line search on smooth objective at q
       accepted=False
       for trial in [step,step/2,...]:
           q=project_simplex(p - trial*grad)
           qb=fft_conv(q); G2,w2=smooth(qb,beta)
           if n*G2 < n*G - 1e-14:
               p_new=q; accepted=True; break
       if not accepted: step*=0.5; if step<1e-8 break
       else: if step<1: step*=1.05
       # heavy ball?
       if use_momentum:
           new_p = p_new + 0.3*(p_new - prev)? project
       prev=p; p=p_new
       exact=2n*max(fft_conv(p)?) Already qb; update global if exact improved.
       if no_improve... break
```

`choose_best` as manual; if we call on POOL, it will sort top but leave others? We'll implement:
```
def prune_pool(keep_count=30):
    global POOL
    selected=[]
    while len(selected)<keep_count and POOL:
       bi=0; best=POOL[0][0]
       for i in range(1,len(POOL)):
           if POOL[i][0] < best:
               best=POOL[i][0]; bi=i
       selected.append(POOL[bi])
       del POOL[bi]
    POOL[:] = selected
```

Start generation details:
```
def generate_basic_starts(n, rng, max_count=24):
    out=[]
    def add? no nested. Just append.
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    p=np.full(n,1/n); out.append(p)
    for a in (-5,-3,-1.5,1.5,3,5): p=np.exp(a*(x-0.5)); normalize; append
    x2=np.clip(x,1e-9,1-1e-9)
    for a,b in [(0.2,0.5),(0.5,0.2),(0.5,2),(2,0.5),(0.1,1),(1,0.1),(0.3,3),(3,0.3),(2,4),(4,2)]: p=x2**a*(1-x2)**b; normalize; append
```

`run_minimize` wrapper:

```
def run_lbfogs_full(p0, n, betas, deadline, maxiter_per_beta):
    c = c_from_p(p0)
    state_traj_score = fast_score(p0)
    state_traj_c = c.copy()
    state_traj_p = p0.copy()
    current_c = c
    for beta in betas:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
        state = {'traj_score': state_traj_score, 'traj_p': state_traj_p, 'traj_c': state_traj_c}
        scale = float(n)
        options = {'maxiter': maxiter_per_beta, 'ftol':1e-10, 'gtol':1e-6, 'maxls':50, 'maxcor':20}
        try:
            res = minimize(full_objective, current_c, args=(n,beta,scale,state), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options=options)
            new_c = np.asarray(res.x,dtype=np.float64)
        except Exception:
            new_c = current_c
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(new_c)):
            new_c = state['traj_c'].copy()
        # update trajectory best if state better
        if state['traj_score'] < state_traj_score:
            state_traj_score=state['traj_score']; state_traj_c=state['traj_c']; state_traj_p=state['traj_p']; add_candidate(state_traj_p, score=state_traj_score)
        # if res not better than current? Use trajectory best for next beta
        current_c = state_traj_c.copy()
    # after done, add_candidate state_traj_p if finite
```

```
def gradient_refine_full(p0, deadline, beta=None, momentum=0.0):
    p=normalize_simplex(p0); n=p.size
    if n<2: return p
    if beta is None: beta=max(10000.0, 10.0*n)
    best=p.copy(); best_score=fast_score(p)
    step=1.0; no_improve=0; prev=p.copy()

       b=fft_convolution(p); G,w=smooth...
       if not finite: break

       else: no_improve+=1
```

```
def adam_refine_full(p0, deadline, beta=None, lr=0.02):
   n=p0.size; c=c_from_p(p0)
   c=np.clip(c,-25,25)
   best_c=c.copy(); best_p=p0.copy(); best_score=fast_score(best_p)
   m=np.zeros(c.size); v=np.zeros(c.size); t=0; no_improve=0
   while time<deadline:
      p=p_from_c(c,n)
      b=fft_conv(p); G,w=smooth...
      score=2n*max(b)
      if score<best_score: best...; no_improve=0; update global; if best_score < target? maybe print
      else no_improve+=1
      g=2*corr(p,w); gp=dot(p,g); grad=p*(g-gp); grad=grad[:-1]
      if not finite: break
      t+=1; m=...; v=...
      c_new=c-lr*mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
      c_new=np.clip(c_new,-25,25)
      # maybe if c_new changes too much? clip?
      c=c_new
      if no_improve>5000: lr*=0.5; no_improve=0; if lr<1e-5: break
   return best_p
```

Detailed functions:

`fft_convolution`:
```
def fft_convolution(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([a[0]*a[0]], dtype=np.float64)
    need = 2*n - 1
    size = 1
    while size < need:
        size <<= 1
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    b = np.fft.irfft(F * F, size)[:need]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    return b
```
Potential negative true? no.

`smooth_max_and_weights`:
```
def smooth_max_and_weights(b, beta):
    b = np.asarray(b,dtype=np.float64)
    if b.size == 0: return inf, empty
    m=float(np.max(b))
    if not finite(m): return inf, zeros
    if m <=0 or beta <=0:
        w=np.ones(b.size)/b.size; return m,w
    x=beta*(b-m)
    x=np.clip(x,-745.0,0.0)
    e=np.exp(x)
    Z=float(np.sum(e))
    if Z<=0 or not finite(Z):
       e=np.zeros(b.size); e[int(np.argmax(b))]=1.0; Z=1.0
    w=e/Z
    G=m + float(np.log(Z))/beta
    return G,w
```

`corr_pw` as above. Ensure w length maybe 2n-1. For n maybe 1. works.

`normalize_simplex`:
```
def normalize_simplex(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if p.size==0: return p
    p=np.where(np.isfinite(p),p,0.0)
    p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
    s=float(np.sum(p))
    if s<=0 or not finite: return full(p.shape,1/p.size)
    return p/s
```

`fast_score`:
```
def fast_score(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=float64); n=p.size
    if n==0: return inf
    s=sum; if s<=0: return inf
    if s not 1? We can normalize? For score scale invariant; if p not sum1, use formula 2n max/(s^2). Use p as is.
    b=fft_conv(p)
    M=max(b)
    return 2*n*M/(s*s)
```
Good, no need normalize. But global best should store normalized.

`project_simplex`: from previous.

`p_from_c`:

```
def p_from_c(c,n):
    c=np.asarray(c,dtype=float64).reshape(-1)
    if n<=0: return empty
    if c.size < n-1: pad
    elif > n-1: truncate
    z=np.empty(n); z[:-1]=c; z[-1]=0
    m=np.max(z); ez=np.exp(z-m); s=sum; if invalid uniform; return ez/s
```

```
return p
```

`c_from_p`:
```
def c_from_p(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=float64)
    n=p.size
    if n<=1: return np.empty(0)
    eps=1e-300
    ps=np.where(np.isfinite(p),p,0.0)
    ps=np.maximum(ps,0.0)
    s=sum(ps)
    if s<=0: return zeros
    ps=ps/s
    ps=np.maximum(ps, eps)
    ps=ps/ps.sum()
    logp=np.log(ps)
    c=logp[:-1]-logp[-1]
    c=np.clip(c, -30.0, 30.0)
    return c
```
Using eps 1e-300; log ~-690; after subtract last maybe huge but clip 30. Good. Clipping may make sum not exactly 1? p_from_c will renormalize; okay.

`resize_nonneg`:
```
def resize_nonneg(arr,n):
    arr=np.asarray(arr,dtype=float64).ravel()
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr),arr,0.0)
    arr=np.maximum(arr,0.0)
    s=arr.sum()
    if arr.size==0 or s<=0: return full(n,1/n)
    arr=arr/s
    if arr.size==n: return arr
    xs=np.linspace(0.1,0.999999? Actually if endpoints zeros maybe use 0,1. Interp at endpoints can be zero. Use 0,1.
    xn=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    interp=np.interp(xn,xs,1.0+arr? To avoid log? No just arr.
    # If arr has sharp spikes, interp may smooth; okay.
    return normalize_simplex(interp)
```
If arr.size=1, linspace [0], interp all arr[0]. good.

`get_reference_sequence`:

```
def get_reference_sequence(n):
    h = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
    if h is None: return None
    arr = resize_nonneg(h,n)
    if arr.size!=n or not finite: return None
    return arr
```

`generate_basic_starts` no nested. Need rng. Use `np.random.Generator` methods.

Let's write:
```
def generate_basic_starts(n, rng, max_count=30):
    out=[]
    if n<=0: return out
    out.append(np.full(n, 1.0/n))
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    xm=x-0.5
    for a in (-6.0,-3.0,-1.5,1.5,3.0,6.0):
       p=np.exp(a*xm); p=normalize_simplex(p); out.append(p)
    xc=np.clip(x,1e-10,1.0-1e-10)
    betas=[(0.2,0.5),(0.5,0.2),(0.25,2.0),(2.0,0.25),(0.1,1.0),(1.0,0.1),(0.2,3.0),(3.0,0.2),(2.0,5.0),(5.0,2.0),(0.5,1.0),(1.0,0.5)]
    for aa,bb in betas:
       p=xc**aa * (1.0-xc)**bb
       p=normalize_simplex(p)
       out.append(p)
    # two-level blocks
    for frac in (0.2,0.35,0.5,0.65,0.8):
       k=int(frac*n)
       if k<=0 or k>=n: continue
       for r in (0.1,0.25,0.6,0.9,1.4,2.0,4.0):
          p=np.ones(n)
          p[:k]=r
          p=normalize_simplex(p)
          out.append(p)
       for r in (0.1,0.25,0.6,0.9,1.4,2.0,4.0):
          p=np.ones(n)
          p[k:]=r
          p=normalize_simplex(p)
          out.append(p)
    # sine
    for freq in (1,2,3,4,6):
       for amp in (0.3,0.6,0.9):
          for phase in (0.0,0.7,1.5):
             p=1.0 + amp*np.sin(2.0*np.pi*freq*x + phase)
             p=np.maximum(p,1e-4)
             out.append(normalize_simplex(p))
    # random smooth
    for cutoff in (2,4,8,16,32):
       z=np.zeros(n)
       for trial in (0,1):
          noise=rng.normal(size=n)
          F=np.fft.rfft(noise)
          size=F.size
          keep=cutoff
          if keep < size:
             F[1:size-keep]=0.0
             if keep+1 < size: F[size-keep:]=0.0? rfft frequencies: indices 0..size-1; low freq first, high last. To keep |k|<=cutoff, zero from cutoff+1 to size-cutoff-1. But zeroing high at end okay.
          inv=np.fft.irfft(F, n)
          inv=inv - inv.mean()
          p=np.exp(1.2*np.tanh(inv))
          out.append(normalize_simplex(p))
    return out[:max_count]
```
Need careful rfft zeroing: `F = np.fft.rfft(noise); half=len(F); keep=min(cutoff, half-1); if keep<half-1: F[keep+1:]=0`. This keeps low and corresponding negative (because real rfft last is Nyquist). Good.

`downsample_blocks(p,n,B)`:
```
def downsample_blocks(p,n,B):
    L=n//B
    q=np.zeros(B)
    for b in range(B): q[b]=p[b*L:(b+1)*L].sum()
    return normalize_simplex(q)
```

```
def add_candidate(p, score=None):
    global GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE, GLOBAL_BEST_P, POOL
    if p is None: return
    pn=normalize_simplex(p)
    if pn.size!=len? Return

        score=fast_score(pn)
    else:
        # validate score finite; if not recompute

            score=fast_score(pn)
    if score < GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE:
        GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE=score; GLOBAL_BEST_P=pn.copy(); print(...)

        prune_pool(40)
```

```
def choose_best_from_pool(count, working=None):
    global POOL
    if working is None:
        working = [item for item in POOL] # No lambda/list comprehension? List comprehension okay? It's not a lambda, but maybe acceptable. To be safe use loop.
    selected=[]
    while len(selected)<count and working:
       bi=0; best=working[0][0]
       for i in range(1,len(working)):
          if working[i][0] < best:
             best=working[i][0]; bi=i
       selected.append(working[bi])
       del working[bi]
    return selected
```

`full_objective` detailed:
```
def full_objective(c,n,beta,scale,state):
    global GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE, GLOBAL_BEST_P
    size = max(0,n-1)
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if c.size != size:
       tmp=np.zeros(size); m=min(size,c.size); tmp[:m]=c[:m]; c=tmp
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
       return 1e30, np.zeros(size)
    p = p_from_c(c,n)
    b = fft_convolution(p)
    if b.size == 0: return 1e30, zeros
    G,w = smooth_max_and_weights(b,beta)
    if not np.isfinite(G): return 1e30, zeros
    g = 2.0*corr_pw(p,w)
    gp = float(np.dot(p,g))
    grad_z = p*(g-gp)
    grad = scale * grad_z[:-1]
    F = scale * float(n*G)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.zeros(size)
        F = 1e30
    score = 2.0*n*float(np.max(b))
    if np.isfinite(score):
       if score < state.get('traj_score', float('inf')):
           state['traj_score']=score; state['traj_p']=p.copy(); state['traj_c']=c.copy()
       if score < GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE - 1e-12:
           GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE=score; GLOBAL_BEST_P=p.copy(); print(...)
    return F, grad
```
If F too high? If nG finite. `scale` float. Good.

`run_lbfogs_full` detailed:
```
def run_lbfogs_full(p0, n, betas, deadline, maxiter):
    if n<=1: return
    p0=normalize_simplex(p0)
    c0=c_from_p(p0)
    c0=np.clip(c0,-30,30)
    # if c0 all very similar? add tiny asym if symmetric? The p0 from starts not symmetric. We can add small random:
    if np.linalg.norm(c0) < 1e-12:
       c0 = 0.05*(np.linspace(0,1,c0.size)-0.5)
    current_c=c0.copy()
    traj_score=fast_score(p0)
    traj_p=p0.copy(); traj_c=c0.copy()
    add_candidate(traj_p, score=traj_score)
    for beta in betas:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       if beta <=0: continue
       state={'traj_score':traj_score,'traj_p':traj_p,'traj_c':traj_c}
       scale=float(n)
       options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-10,'gtol':1e-6,'maxls':50,'maxcor':20}
       try:
          res=minimize(full_objective, current_c, args=(n,beta,scale,state), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options=options)
          new_c=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=np.float64)
       except Exception:
          new_c=current_c
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(new_c)):
          new_c=current_c
       # Update trajectory from state
       if state['traj_score'] < traj_score:
          traj_score=state['traj_score']; traj_p=state['traj_p']; traj_c=state['traj_c']
          add_candidate(traj_p, score=traj_score)
       # Also evaluate new_c maybe state did if objective called at final? It may not; do add_candidate p_from_c(new_c)
       p_new=p_from_c(new_c,n)
       p_new=normalize_simplex(p_new)
       add_candidate(p_new) # rescore
       # Choose next current_c: if state['traj_c'] better than current? state['traj_c'] from best exact. Use it.
       if state.get('traj_c') is not None:
          current_c=state['traj_c'].copy()
       else:
          current_c=new_c
       current_c=np.clip(current_c,-40,40)
    add_candidate(traj_p, score=traj_score)
```
But `add_candidate(p_new)` may score many times (each beta). Fine. `state['traj_c']` might be old from previous beta if no improvements; okay.

```
def run_lbfogs_block(n,B,betas,deadline,maxiter):
```

```
       cur=n*G
       exact=2n*max(b)
       if exact < GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE - 1e-12: update global
       no_improve...
       g=2corr ; gp; grad=g-gp
       # line search multipliers
       accepted=False
       for mult in (1,0.5,...):
          q=project_simplex(p - step*mult*grad)
          qb=fft_conv(q); G2,_=smooth(qb,beta)
          if n*G2 < cur - 1e-13:
             p=q; accepted=True; cur=n*G2; break
       if not accepted:
          step*=0.5; if step<1e-8 break
       else:
          if step<1: step*=1.05
       no_improve logic based exact maybe compute every 20
       it+=1
       if it%1000 print
    update global exact final
```

Implement `adam_refine_full(p0, deadline, beta=None, lr=0.01)`:
```
def adam_refine_full(p0, deadline, beta=None, lr=0.01):
    n=p0.size
    c=c_from_p(p0)
    c=np.clip(c,-25,25)
    best_p=p_from_c(c,n); best_score=fast_score(best_p); if best_score<global update
    m=np.zeros(c.size); v=np.zeros(c.size); t=0; no_improve=0; current_lr=lr
    while time<deadline:
       p=p_from_c(c,n)
       b=fft_conv(p); G,w=smooth...
       score=2n*max(b)
       if score < best_score - 1e-12:
          best_score=score; best_p=p.copy(); update global; no_improve=0
       else:
          no_improve+=1
       if no_improve > 5000:
          current_lr *= 0.5
          no_improve=0
          if current_lr < 1e-5: break
       if not finite: break
       g=2corr; gp=dot; grad=p*(g-gp); grad=grad[:-1]
       if not finite: break
       t+=1
       m=0.9*m+0.1*grad
       v=0.999*v+0.001*grad*grad
       mhat=m/(1-0.9**t)
       vhat=v/(1-0.999**t)
       c_new=c - current_lr*mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
       c_new=np.clip(c_new,-25,25)
       c=c_new
    # final update best if exact?
    if best_p is not None: add_candidate(best_p, best_score)
```

```
def prune_pool(keep_count=40):
    global POOL
    selected=[]
    while len(selected) < keep_count and len(POOL) > 0:
        bi=0; best=POOL[0][0]
        for i in range(1,len(POOL)):
            if POOL[i][0] < best:
```

```
def p_from_c(c, n):
    if n <= 0: return np.empty(0)
    if n == 1: return np.array([1.0])
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    need = n - 1
    if c.size != need:
        tmp = np.zeros(need, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(need, c.size)
        tmp[:m] = c[:m]
        c = tmp
    z = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    z[:-1] = c
    z[-1] = 0.0
    m = float(np.max(z))
    ez = np.exp(z - m)
    s = float(np.sum(ez))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(n, 1.0/n)
    return ez/s
```
If c has nonfinite, `np.max` inf; but objective checks. Could still guard:


```
def fast_score(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64)
    p=np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
    n=p.size
    if n==0: return inf
    s=float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 1e-300: return inf
    b=fft_convolution(p)
    M=float(np.max(b))
    return 2.0*n*M/(s*s)
```

Implementation:
```
def choose_best_from_pool(count):
    working=[]
    for item in POOL:
        working.append(item)
    selected=[]
    while len(selected) < count and len(working) > 0:
       bi=0; best=working[0][0]
       for i in range(1,len(working)):
          if working[i][0] < best: best=working[i][0]; bi=i
       selected.append(working[bi])
       del working[bi]
    return selected
```

```python
"""
Search for low-peak self-convolution nonnegative sequences.

Minimize 2*n*max(a*a)/(sum(a))^2.  The search normalizes sum(a)=1 and
minimizes max(a*a).  It combines:
  - coarse piecewise-constant L-BFGS optimization,
  - full-length L-BFGS optimization in log-softmax coordinates,
  - projected-gradient polish,
  - Adam-style refinement in log-softmax coordinates,
  - multiple structured and random smooth starts.

The function propose_candidate returns a list of nonnegative floats.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
    HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_SCIPY = False


GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE = float("inf")
GLOBAL_BEST_P = None
GLOBAL_LAST_PRINT = -1.0
POOL = []
BOUND_C = 35.0


def fft_convolution(a):
    """Linear convolution a*a using FFT. Clips tiny negative FFT roundoff."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([a[0] * a[0]], dtype=np.float64)

    need = 2 * n - 1
    size = 1
    while size < need:
        size <<= 1

    F = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    b = np.fft.irfft(F * F, size)[:need]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    return b


def smooth_max_and_weights(b, beta):
    """
    Smooth max of b and normalized softmax weights.

    Returns G = beta-logsumexp(b)/beta and weights w_k proportional to
    exp(beta*(b_k-max(b))).
    """
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    if b.size == 0:
        return float("inf"), np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    m = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf"), np.zeros_like(b, dtype=np.float64)

    if m <= 0.0 or beta <= 0.0:
        w = np.ones_like(b, dtype=np.float64)
        w /= float(b.size)
        return m, w

    x = beta * (b - m)
    x = np.clip(x, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(x)
    Z = float(np.sum(e))

    if Z <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(Z):
        e = np.zeros_like(b, dtype=np.float64)
        e[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0
        Z = 1.0

    w = e / Z
    G = m + float(np.log(Z)) / beta
    return float(G), w


def corr_pw(p, w):
    """
    Return s_i = sum_j p_j w_{i+j}, for i=0..n-1.

    This is the derivative helper for max(p*p) under softmax weights w.
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    m = w.size
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.zeros(max(n, 0), dtype=np.float64)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)) or not np.all(np.isfinite(w)):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    need = n + m - 1
    size = 1
    while size < need:
        size <<= 1

    P = np.fft.rfft(p, size)
    WR = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], size)
    C = np.fft.irfft(P * WR, size)[:need]

    # s_i = C[m-1-i], i=0..n-1  =>  C[m-n:m] reversed
    s = C[m - n:m][::-1].copy()
    return np.ascontiguousarray(s, dtype=np.float64)


def normalize_simplex(p):
    """Clip to nonnegative and renormalize to sum 1."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if p.size == 0:
        return p
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(p.shape, 1.0 / float(p.size), dtype=np.float64)
    p *= 1.0 / s
    return p


def fast_score(p):
    """
    Fast FFT estimate of the official score.
    Scale invariant: uses 2*n*max(conv)/(sum)^2.
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")

    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return float("inf")

    b = fft_convolution(p)
    if b.size == 0:
        return float("inf")

    M = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(M):
        return float("inf")

    return float(2.0 * n * M / (s * s))


def update_global_sequence(p, score):
    """Update global best, with a light print throttle."""
    global GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE, GLOBAL_BEST_P, GLOBAL_LAST_PRINT

    if p is None:
        return
    try:
        score = float(score)
    except Exception:
        return

    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return

    pn = normalize_simplex(p)
    if pn.size == 0:
        return

    if score < GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE - 1e-12:
        GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE = score
        GLOBAL_BEST_P = pn.copy()
        if score < GLOBAL_LAST_PRINT - 1e-5:
            print(f"global best score={score:.9f}, len={pn.size}")
            GLOBAL_LAST_PRINT = score


def add_candidate(p, score=None):
    """Add a candidate sequence to the pool and update global best."""
    global POOL

    if p is None:
        return float("inf")

    pn = normalize_simplex(p)
    if pn.size == 0:
        return float("inf")

    if score is None:
        score = fast_score(pn)
    else:
        try:
            score = float(score)
        except Exception:
            score = fast_score(pn)
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            score = fast_score(pn)

    update_global_sequence(pn, score)

    POOL.append([float(score), pn.copy()])
    if len(POOL) > 80:
        prune_pool(40)

    return float(score)


def prune_pool(keep_count=40):
    """Reduce POOL to the best keep_count sequences."""
    global POOL

    selected = []
    while len(selected) < keep_count and len(POOL) > 0:
        bi = 0
        best = POOL[0][0]
        for i in range(1, len(POOL)):
            if POOL[i][0] < best:
                best = POOL[i][0]
                bi = i
        selected.append(POOL[bi])
        del POOL[bi]

    POOL[:] = selected


def choose_best_from_pool(count):
    """Return up to count best (score, p) entries from POOL, without removing them."""
    working = []
    for item in POOL:
        working.append(item)

    selected = []
    while len(selected) < count and len(working) > 0:
        bi = 0
        best = working[0][0]
        for i in range(1, len(working)):
            if working[i][0] < best:
                best = working[i][0]
                bi = i
        selected.append(working[bi])
        del working[bi]

    return selected


def p_from_c(c, n):
    """
    p = softmax(z), where z[:-1]=c and z[-1]=0.
    This parameterizes the simplex with n-1 free log-weights.
    """
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)

    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    need = n - 1
    if c.size != need:
        tmp = np.zeros(need, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(need, c.size)
        tmp[:m] = c[:m]
        c = tmp

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        c = np.zeros(need, dtype=np.float64)

    z = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    z[:-1] = c
    z[-1] = 0.0

    mx = float(np.max(z))
    ez = np.exp(z - mx)
    s = float(np.sum(ez))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)

    return ez / s


def c_from_p(p):
    """Convert positive simplex vector p to log-ratio c with last coordinate fixed to 0."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    if n <= 1:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    ps = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    ps = np.maximum(ps, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(ps))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)

    ps = ps / s
    ps = np.maximum(ps, 1e-300)
    ps = ps / float(np.sum(ps))
    logp = np.log(ps)
    c = logp[:-1] - logp[-1]
    c = np.clip(c, -30.0, 30.0)
    return c.astype(np.float64)


def block_to_p(n, B, c):
    """Piecewise-constant sequence from block log-weights c."""
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    if B <= 1:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)

    if n % B != 0:
        B = 1
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)

    L = n // B
    q = p_from_c(c, B)
    p = np.repeat(q / float(L), L)
    return p


def project_simplex(v, target=1.0):
    """Project v onto {x >= 0, sum(x)=target}."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    if v.size == 0:
        return v

    if target <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros_like(v, dtype=np.float64)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        return np.full(v.shape, target / float(v.size), dtype=np.float64)

    low = float(np.min(v)) - 1.0
    high = float(np.max(v)) + 1.0

    for _ in range(60):
        mid = 0.5 * (low + high)
        s = float(np.sum(np.maximum(v - mid, 0.0)))
        if s > target:
            low = mid
        else:
            high = mid

    tau = 0.5 * (low + high)
    q = np.maximum(v - tau, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(q))

    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        q = np.full(v.shape, target / float(v.size), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        q *= target / s

    return q


def resize_nonneg(arr, n):
    """Resize a nonnegative sequence to length n by linear interpolation."""
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if arr.size == 0 or s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)

    arr = arr / s
    if arr.size == n:
        return arr.copy()

    if arr.size == 1:
        return np.full(n, arr[0], dtype=np.float64)

    xs = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    yy = np.interp(xn, xs, arr)
    return normalize_simplex(yy)


def get_reference_sequence(n):
    """Use height_sequence_1 if available; otherwise return None."""
    h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if h is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = resize_nonneg(h, n)
        if arr.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
            return None
        return arr
    except Exception:
        return None


def downsample_blocks(p, n, B):
    """Average each block of length n//B."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    if B <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    L = n // B
    if L <= 0:
        return normalize_simplex(np.full(B, 1.0 / float(B)))

    q = np.zeros(B, dtype=np.float64)
    for b in range(B):
        q[b] = float(np.sum(p[b * L:(b + 1) * L]))
    return normalize_simplex(q)


def generate_basic_starts(n, rng, max_count=30):
    """Generate a diverse set of nonnegative starting sequences."""
    if n <= 0:
        return []
    if n == 1:
        return [np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)]

    out = []
    out.append(np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64))

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    xm = x - 0.5

    # Exponential ramps.
    for a in (-6.0, -3.0, -1.5, 1.5, 3.0, 6.0):
        p = np.exp(a * xm)
        out.append(normalize_simplex(p))

    # Cubic asymmetry.
    xm3 = xm * xm * xm
    for a in (-8.0, -4.0, 4.0, 8.0):
        p = np.exp(a * xm3)
        out.append(normalize_simplex(p))

    xc = np.clip(x, 1e-10, 1.0 - 1e-10)
    beta_pairs = (
        (0.2, 0.5), (0.5, 0.2),
        (0.25, 2.0), (2.0, 0.25),
        (0.1, 1.0), (1.0, 0.1),
        (0.2, 3.0), (3.0, 0.2),
        (2.0, 5.0), (5.0, 2.0),
        (0.5, 1.0), (1.0, 0.5),
    )
    for aa, bb in beta_pairs:
        p = (xc ** aa) * ((1.0 - xc) ** bb)
        out.append(normalize_simplex(p))

    # Two-level blocks.
    for frac in (0.2, 0.35, 0.5, 0.65, 0.8):
        k = int(frac * n)
        if k <= 0 or k >= n:
            continue
        for r in (0.1, 0.25, 0.6, 0.9, 1.4, 2.0, 4.0):
            p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            p[:k] = r
            out.append(normalize_simplex(p))
        for r in (0.1, 0.25, 0.6, 0.9, 1.4, 2.0, 4.0):
            p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            p[k:] = r
            out.append(normalize_simplex(p))

    # Sine modulations.
    for freq in (1, 2, 3, 4, 6):
        for amp in (0.3, 0.6, 0.9):
            for phase in (0.0, 0.7, 1.5):
                p = 1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x + phase)
                p = np.maximum(p, 1e-4)
                out.append(normalize_simplex(p))

    # Low-frequency random smooth profiles.
    if n >= 16:
        for cutoff in (2, 4, 8, 16, 32):
            for _ in range(2):
                noise = rng.normal(size=n)
                F = np.fft.rfft(noise)
                half = F.size
                keep = min(cutoff, half - 1)
                if keep < half - 1:
                    F[keep + 1:] = 0.0
                z = np.fft.irfft(F, n)
                z = z - float(np.mean(z))
                p = np.exp(1.2 * np.tanh(z))
                out.append(normalize_simplex(p))

    return out[:max_count]


def lowpass_nonneg(p, cutoff):
    """Apply a mild real FFT low-pass filter, then make nonnegative and normalize."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    if n <= 1:
        return normalize_simplex(p)

    F = np.fft.rfft(p)
    half = F.size
    keep = min(cutoff, half - 1)
    if keep < half - 1:
        F[keep + 1:] = 0.0

    q = np.fft.irfft(F, n)
    q = np.where(np.isfinite(q), q, 0.0)
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    return normalize_simplex(q)


def full_objective(c, n, beta, scale, state):
    """
    L-BFGS objective/gradient for full-length log-softmax variables.

    Minimizes n * smooth_max(p*p).  The gradient is scaled by scale (n)
    so that the gradient norm is O(1).
    """
    global GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE, GLOBAL_BEST_P, GLOBAL_LAST_PRINT

    size = max(0, n - 1)
    if size == 0:
        return 1e30, np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if c.size != size:
        tmp = np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(size, c.size)
        tmp[:m] = c[:m]
        c = tmp

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        return 1e30, np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)

    c_use = np.clip(c, -BOUND_C, BOUND_C)
    p = p_from_c(c_use, n)
    if p.size != n:
        return 1e30, np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)

    b = fft_convolution(p)
    if b.size == 0:
        return 1e30, np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)

    G, w = smooth_max_and_weights(b, beta)
    if not np.isfinite(G):
        return 1e30, np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)

    cor = corr_pw(p, w)
    # True gradient of n*G with respect to p.
    g = float(n) * 2.0 * cor
    gp = float(np.dot(p, g))
    grad_z = p * (g - gp)
    grad = float(scale) * grad_z[:-1]

    mask = (c > BOUND_C) | (c < -BOUND_C)
    if np.any(mask):
        grad = grad.copy()
        grad[mask] = 0.0

    F = float(scale) * float(n) * float(G)
    if not np.isfinite(F) or not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        return 1e30, np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)

    score = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(b))
    if np.isfinite(score):
        if score < state.get("traj_score", float("inf")) - 1e-15:
            state["traj_score"] = float(score)
            state["traj_p"] = p.copy()
            state["traj_c"] = c_use.copy()

        if score < GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE - 1e-12:
            GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE = float(score)
            GLOBAL_BEST_P = p.copy()
            if score < GLOBAL_LAST_PRINT - 1e-5:
                print(f"global best score={score:.9f}, len={n}")
                GLOBAL_LAST_PRINT = score

    return F, grad


def block_objective(c, n, B, beta, scale, state):
    """
    L-BFGS objective/gradient for piecewise-constant log-weights.

    p[i] = q_block / L.  q is parameterized by c with last coordinate fixed.
    """
    global GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE, GLOBAL_BEST_P, GLOBAL_LAST_PRINT

    if B <= 1:
        return 1e30, np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    size = B - 1
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if c.size != size:
        tmp = np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(size, c.size)
        tmp[:m] = c[:m]
        c = tmp

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        return 1e30, np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)

    c_use = np.clip(c, -BOUND_C, BOUND_C)
    q = p_from_c(c_use, B)
    L = n // B
    if L <= 0:
        return 1e30, np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)

    p = np.repeat(q / float(L), L)
    if p.size != n:
        return 1e30, np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)

    b = fft_convolution(p)
    if b.size == 0:
        return 1e30, np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)

    G, w = smooth_max_and_weights(b, beta)
    if not np.isfinite(G):
        return 1e30, np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)

    cor = corr_pw(p, w)
    # True gradient of n*G wrt fine p.
    g = float(n) * 2.0 * cor

    # Aggregate to block derivatives: sum over block and divide by L.
    gb = g.reshape(B, L).sum(axis=1) / float(L)
    gp = float(np.dot(q, gb))
    grad_z = q * (gb - gp)
    grad = float(scale) * grad_z[:-1]

    mask = (c > BOUND_C) | (c < -BOUND_C)
    if np.any(mask):
        grad = grad.copy()
        grad[mask] = 0.0

    F = float(scale) * float(n) * float(G)
    if not np.isfinite(F) or not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        return 1e30, np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)

    score = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(b))
    if np.isfinite(score):
        if score < state.get("traj_score", float("inf")) - 1e-15:
            state["traj_score"] = float(score)
            state["traj_p"] = p.copy()
            state["traj_c"] = c_use.copy()

        if score < GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE - 1e-12:
            GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE = float(score)
            GLOBAL_BEST_P = p.copy()
            if score < GLOBAL_LAST_PRINT - 1e-5:
                print(f"global best score={score:.9f}, len={n}")
                GLOBAL_LAST_PRINT = score

    return F, grad


def run_lbfogs_block(n, B, betas, deadline, maxiter, rng):
    """Optimize piecewise-constant block weights for a fixed number of blocks."""
    if not HAVE_SCIPY or B <= 1 or n % B != 0:
        return

    size = B - 1
    if size <= 0:
        return

    c_candidates = []

    # Uniform.
    c_candidates.append(np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64))

    # Reference sequence if available.
    ref = get_reference_sequence(n)
    if ref is not None:
        q = resize_nonneg(ref, B)
        c = c_from_p(q)
        c = np.clip(c, -30.0, 30.0)
        c_candidates.append(c)

    # Current global best, downsampled.
    if GLOBAL_BEST_P is not None and GLOBAL_BEST_P.size == n:
        q = downsample_blocks(GLOBAL_BEST_P, n, B)
        c = c_from_p(q)
        c = np.clip(c, -30.0, 30.0)
        c_candidates.append(c)

    # Structured starts at block resolution.
    basic = generate_basic_starts(B, rng, max_count=12)
    for p in basic:
        c = c_from_p(p)
        c = np.clip(c, -30.0, 30.0)
        c_candidates.append(c)

    # Downsample some full-length candidates.
    top = choose_best_from_pool(3)
    for _, p in top:
        if p.size != n:
            continue
        q = downsample_blocks(p, n, B)
        c = c_from_p(q)
        c = np.clip(c, -30.0, 30.0)
        c_candidates.append(c)

    # Score and keep a few best block starts.
    scored = []
    for c in c_candidates:
        p = block_to_p(n, B, c)
        sc = fast_score(p)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append([float(sc), c.copy(), p.copy()])

    if len(scored) == 0:
        return

    keep = 4 if B <= 256 else 2 if B <= 1024 else 1
    top = []
    while len(top) < keep and len(scored) > 0:
        bi = 0
        best = scored[0][0]
        for i in range(1, len(scored)):
            if scored[i][0] < best:
                best = scored[i][0]
                bi = i
        top.append(scored[bi])
        del scored[bi]

    options = {
        "maxiter": int(max(50, maxiter)),
        "ftol": 1e-10,
        "gtol": 1e-6,
        "maxls": 50,
        "maxcor": max(2, min(20, size)),
    }

    for sc0, c0, p0 in top:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break

        current_c = np.asarray(c0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        traj_score = float(sc0)
        traj_p = np.asarray(p0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        traj_c = current_c.copy()
        add_candidate(traj_p, score=traj_score)

        for beta in betas:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break
            if beta <= 0:
                continue

            state = {
                "traj_score": traj_score,
                "traj_p": traj_p.copy(),
                "traj_c": traj_c.copy(),
            }
            scale = float(B)

            try:
                res = minimize(
                    block_objective,
                    current_c,
                    args=(n, B, beta, scale, state),
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    jac=True,
                    options=options,
                )
                new_c = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
                success = bool(getattr(res, "success", False))
            except Exception:
                new_c = current_c
                success = False

            if not np.all(np.isfinite(new_c)):
                new_c = current_c
                success = False

            if state["traj_score"] < traj_score:
                traj_score = state["traj_score"]
                traj_p = state["traj_p"].copy()
                traj_c = state["traj_c"].copy()
                add_candidate(traj_p, score=traj_score)

            p_new = p_from_c(new_c, B)
            # Reconstruct full length for exact scoring/candidate.
            p_new_full = block_to_p(n, B, new_c)
            sc_new = add_candidate(p_new_full)

            if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < traj_score + 0.02:
                current_c = new_c
            else:
                current_c = traj_c.copy()

            current_c = np.clip(current_c, -BOUND_C, BOUND_C)

            if not success and time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break

        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        add_candidate(traj_p, score=traj_score)


def run_lbfogs_full(p0, n, betas, deadline, maxiter):
    """Optimize full-length log-softmax coordinates from a given start."""
    if not HAVE_SCIPY or p0 is None:
        return

    p0 = normalize_simplex(p0)
    if p0.size != n or n <= 1:
        return

    c0 = c_from_p(p0)
    c0 = np.clip(c0, -30.0, 30.0)

    # Break exact symmetry if starting at uniform.
    if c0.size > 0 and float(np.linalg.norm(c0)) < 1e-12:
        ramp = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, c0.size, dtype=np.float64)
        c0 = c0 + 0.05 * (ramp - 0.5)
        c0 = np.clip(c0, -30.0, 30.0)

    current_c = c0.copy()
    traj_score = fast_score(p0)
    if not np.isfinite(traj_score):
        traj_score = float("inf")
    traj_p = p0.copy()
    traj_c = current_c.copy()
    add_candidate(traj_p, score=traj_score)

    size = max(0, n - 1)
    options = {
        "maxiter": int(max(30, maxiter)),
        "ftol": 1e-10,
        "gtol": 1e-6,
        "maxls": 50,
        "maxcor": max(2, min(20, size)),
    }

    for beta in betas:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        if beta <= 0:
            continue

        state = {
            "traj_score": traj_score,
            "traj_p": traj_p.copy(),
            "traj_c": traj_c.copy(),
        }
        scale = float(n)

        try:
            res = minimize(
                full_objective,
                current_c,
                args=(n, beta, scale, state),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                options=options,
            )
            new_c = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            success = bool(getattr(res, "success", False))
        except Exception:
            new_c = current_c
            success = False

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(new_c)):
            new_c = current_c
            success = False

        if state["traj_score"] < traj_score:
            traj_score = state["traj_score"]
            traj_p = state["traj_p"].copy()
            traj_c = state["traj_c"].copy()
            add_candidate(traj_p, score=traj_score)

        p_new = p_from_c(new_c, n)
        p_new = normalize_simplex(p_new)
        sc_new = add_candidate(p_new)

        if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < traj_score + 0.02:
            current_c = new_c
        else:
            current_c = traj_c.copy()

        current_c = np.clip(current_c, -BOUND_C, BOUND_C)

        if not success and time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break

    add_candidate(traj_p, score=traj_score)


def gradient_refine_full(p0, deadline, beta=None):
    """
    Time-controlled projected-gradient refinement in probability-simplex space.

    Uses the raw smooth objective G (not n*G), so the natural step size is O(1).
    """
    if p0 is None:
        return

    p = normalize_simplex(p0)
    n = p.size
    if n < 2:
        return

    if beta is None:
        beta = max(10000.0, 10.0 * float(n))

    local_best = fast_score(p)
    if local_best < GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE - 1e-12:
        update_global_sequence(p, local_best)

    step = 1.0
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        b = fft_convolution(p)
        G, w = smooth_max_and_weights(b, beta)
        if not np.isfinite(G):
            break

        cur_smooth = float(G)
        exact = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(b))

        if np.isfinite(exact) and exact < local_best - 1e-12:
            local_best = exact
            no_improve = 0
            update_global_sequence(p, exact)
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > 3000:
            break

        cor = corr_pw(p, w)
        g = 2.0 * cor
        gm = float(np.mean(g))
        grad = g - gm
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if not np.isfinite(gnorm) or gnorm < 1e-16:
            break

        accepted = False
        for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005, 0.002, 0.001):
            alpha = step * mult
            q = project_simplex(p - alpha * grad, 1.0)
            qb = fft_convolution(q)
            G2, _ = smooth_max_and_weights(qb, beta)
            if not np.isfinite(G2):
                continue
            if float(G2) < cur_smooth - 1e-14:
                accepted = True
                p = q
                exact_new = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(qb))
                if np.isfinite(exact_new) and exact_new < local_best - 1e-12:
                    local_best = exact_new
                    no_improve = 0
                    update_global_sequence(p, local_best)
                break

        if not accepted:
            step *= 0.5
            if step < 1e-12:
                break
        else:
            if step < 1.0:
                step = min(2.0, step * 1.05)

        it += 1
        if it % 1000 == 0:
            print(f"gradient refine it={it}, local_best={local_best:.9f}, step={step:.3e}")


def adam_refine_full(p0, deadline, beta=None, lr=0.01):
    """
    Time-controlled Adam refinement in log-softmax coordinates.

    Used only as a polish/exploration step.  The best trajectory is retained.
    """
    if p0 is None:
        return

    p0 = normalize_simplex(p0)
    n = p0.size
    if n <= 1:
        return

    if beta is None:
        beta = max(20000.0, 20.0 * float(n))

    c = c_from_p(p0)
    c = np.clip(c, -BOUND_C, BOUND_C)

    best_p = p_from_c(c, n)
    best_p = normalize_simplex(best_p)
    best_score = fast_score(best_p)
    if best_score < GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE - 1e-12:
        update_global_sequence(best_p, best_score)
    best_c = c.copy()

    m = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    current_lr = float(lr)
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if c.size == 0:
            break

        p = p_from_c(c, n)
        b = fft_convolution(p)
        G, w = smooth_max_and_weights(b, beta)
        if not np.isfinite(G):
            c = best_c.copy()
            break

        score = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(b))
        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = float(score)
            best_p = p.copy()
            best_c = c.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            update_global_sequence(best_p, best_score)
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > 5000:
            current_lr *= 0.5
            no_improve = 0
            if current_lr < 1e-5:
                break
            c = best_c.copy()
            m = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
            v = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
            t = 0

        cor = corr_pw(p, w)
        g = 2.0 * cor
        gp = float(np.dot(p, g))
        grad = p * (g - gp)
        if grad.size > 0:
            grad = grad[:-1]

        if grad.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
            break

        t += 1
        b1 = 0.9
        b2 = 0.999
        eps = 1e-8

        m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * grad
        v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * (grad * grad)
        mhat = m / (1.0 - b1 ** t)
        vhat = v / (1.0 - b2 ** t)

        c_new = c - current_lr * mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + eps)
        c = np.clip(c_new, -BOUND_C, BOUND_C)

        it += 1
        if it % 2000 == 0:
            print(f"adam refine it={it}, best={best_score:.9f}, lr={current_lr:.3e}")

    if best_p is not None and best_score < GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE - 1e-12:
        update_global_sequence(best_p, best_score)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    """
    Search for the best sequence and return it as a Python list of nonnegative floats.
    """
    global GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE, GLOBAL_BEST_P, GLOBAL_LAST_PRINT, POOL

    try:
        seed = int(seed)
    except Exception:
        seed = 42

    try:
        budget_s = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget_s = 2400.0

    if budget_s <= 0.0:
        return [1.0]

    start_time = time.monotonic()
    reserve = 90.0
    if budget_s < 300.0:
        reserve = 20.0

    usable = max(0.0, budget_s - reserve)
    deadline = start_time + usable

    if budget_s >= 2000.0:
        n = 32768
    elif budget_s >= 900.0:
        n = 16384
    elif budget_s >= 300.0:
        n = 8192
    else:
        n = 4096

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Reset globals for fresh search.
    GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE = float("inf")
    GLOBAL_BEST_P = None
    GLOBAL_LAST_PRINT = -1.0
    POOL = []

    uniform = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    add_candidate(uniform)

    # Structured full starts.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 10.0:
        full_starts = generate_basic_starts(n, rng, max_count=24)
        for p in full_starts:
            add_candidate(p)

        ref = get_reference_sequence(n)
        if ref is not None:
            add_candidate(ref)
            rev = ref[::-1].copy()
            add_candidate(rev)
            avg = 0.5 * (ref + rev)
            add_candidate(avg)

    # Coarse block optimization.
    if HAVE_SCIPY and time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
        block_list = (8, 16, 32, 64, 128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048, 4096)
        block_betas = (
            max(20.0, float(n) / 500.0),
            max(100.0, float(n) / 100.0),
            max(500.0, float(n) / 20.0),
            max(2000.0, float(n) / 5.0),
            max(10000.0, float(n)),
            max(50000.0, 10.0 * float(n)),
        )

        # Allocate a modest fraction of the budget to blocks.
        block_deadline = start_time + max(60.0, 0.35 * usable)
        if block_deadline > deadline - 120.0:
            block_deadline = deadline - 120.0
        if block_deadline < start_time + 30.0:
            block_deadline = start_time + 30.0

        for B in block_list:
            if time.monotonic() >= block_deadline:
                break
            if B >= n:
                continue
            if n % B != 0:
                continue
            if budget_s >= 2000.0 and B > 4096:
                break
            if budget_s < 900.0 and B > 512:
                break
            if budget_s < 300.0 and B > 128:
                break

            if B <= 64:
                maxit = 250
            elif B <= 256:
                maxit = 180
            elif B <= 1024:
                maxit = 120
            else:
                maxit = 80

            print(f"block optimization B={B}, n={n}, budget_left={max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic()):.1f}s")
            run_lbfogs_block(n, B, block_betas, block_deadline, maxit, rng)

        prune_pool(50)

    # Full L-BFGS refinement from the best coarse/structured starts.
    if HAVE_SCIPY and time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
        full_betas = (
            max(100.0, float(n) / 20.0),
            max(500.0, float(n) / 5.0),
            max(2000.0, float(n)),
            max(10000.0, 10.0 * float(n)),
            max(50000.0, 40.0 * float(n)),
        )

        if budget_s >= 2000.0:
            n_starts = 4
        elif budget_s >= 800.0:
            n_starts = 3
        else:
            n_starts = 2

        top = choose_best_from_pool(n_starts)
        fracs = (0.45, 0.30, 0.15, 0.10)

        for idx, (_, p0) in enumerate(top):
            remaining = deadline - time.monotonic() - 30.0
            if remaining <= 30.0:
                break

            frac = fracs[idx] if idx < len(fracs) else 0.10
            alloc = remaining * frac
            sub_deadline = time.monotonic() + alloc

            maxit = 80 if budget_s >= 1200.0 else 50
            print(f"full L-BFGS start {idx + 1}, remaining_alloc={alloc:.1f}s")
            run_lbfogs_full(p0, n, full_betas, sub_deadline, maxit)

    # Time-controlled polishes.
    if GLOBAL_BEST_P is not None:
        bp = normalize_simplex(GLOBAL_BEST_P)
        if bp.size == n:
            if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0:
                grad_deadline = min(deadline - 30.0, time.monotonic() + 240.0)
                beta_grad = max(10000.0, 10.0 * float(n))
                gradient_refine_full(bp, grad_deadline, beta=beta_grad)

            if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0:
                adam_deadline = min(deadline - 10.0, time.monotonic() + 300.0)
                beta_adam = max(20000.0, 20.0 * float(n))
                adam_refine_full(bp, adam_deadline, beta=beta_adam, lr=0.01)

    # Gentle low-pass candidates: often reduce fine ripple without hurting the shape.
    if GLOBAL_BEST_P is not None:
        bp = normalize_simplex(GLOBAL_BEST_P)
        for cutoff in (2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break
            q = lowpass_nonneg(bp, cutoff)
            add_candidate(q)

    if GLOBAL_BEST_P is None:
        final_p = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        final_p = normalize_simplex(GLOBAL_BEST_P)
        if final_p.size != n:
            final_p = resize_nonneg(final_p, n)

    final_p = np.where(np.isfinite(final_p), final_p, 0.0)
    final_p = np.maximum(final_p, 0.0)
    final_p = np.minimum(final_p, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(final_p))
    if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
        final_p *= 1.0 / s
    else:
        final_p = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)

    final_score = fast_score(final_p)
    print(f"final returned len={final_p.size}, fast_score={final_score:.9f}")

    out = []
    for val in final_p:
        out.append(float(val))
    return out
```
